🤖 RAG ANSWER (45.4s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   To apply for the Erasmus+ Internship Mobility program, you must first
   obtain an acceptance letter from a host organization for a minimum
   internship duration of two months. Undergraduate students need a minimum
   GPA of 2.20, while graduate students require a minimum GPA of 2.50.
   Applications open at the end of October, and you must submit your CV,
   transcript, and acceptance letter through the Online Application System to
   the Internship Office. Your application will be evaluated based on an
   Erasmus+ Score determined by your GPA and language proficiency, and
   selected students are notified by the end of April. Before starting your
   internship, you must complete and submit the Erasmus+ Internship Mobility
   Agreement, Learning Agreement, Grant Agreement, and Travel and Insurance
   documents, all of which must be approved and uploaded via SUFORM.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (78.8s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Erasmus öğrenim hareketliliğinde hibe, katılımcının yurtdışında geçirdiği
   aylar sayısı ile ilgili ev sahibi ülke için belirlenen aylık hibe
   miktarının çarpımıyla hesaplanır. Tamamlanmayan aylar için mali destek, o
   ayda geçirilen gün sayısı ile aylık birim masrafın 1/30’u çarpılarak
   hesaplanır. Hibenin toplam tutarı, hibe sözleşmesinde belirtilen süre ve
   aylık tutarla sınırlıdır. Katılımcı, hareketlilik süresinin tamamı için
   hibe alıyorsa, bu süre en az 3 ay veya bir akademik dönem olmalıdır;
   sıfır-hibe dönemleri varsa, bu süreler ayrı ayrı belirtilir ve mali destek
   yalnızca hibe alan dönemlerde ödenir.
------------------------------------------------------------

   Exchange students must prepare, sign, and submit a Learning Agreement that
   specifies the courses they will take abroad, including their ECTS credits
   and equivalent course types at Sabancı University, with approval from
   their exchange academic advisor. Undergraduate and graduate students must
   complete this process within the timeline announced by the International
   Relations Office, ensuring the agreement aligns with Sabancı University’s
   graduation requirements. The student is responsible for obtaining the
   partner institution’s signature on the agreement and submitting the signed
   copy to the International Relations Office. Additionally, students must
   inform their advisor of any course changes before the term begins and
   secure approval under the Learning Agreement framework. For Erasmus+
   participants, the signed Learning Agreement is required to receive the
   first installment of the grant before departure.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (55.3s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   A student may object to disciplinary sanctions within fifteen days of
   notification, by submitting a petition to the University Administrative
   Board, which must conclude the objection within fifteen days. If the
   objection is rejected, the original disciplinary board or supervisor
   revises the decision based on the grounds for rejection. Students also
   have the right to appeal directly to administrative jurisdiction without
   first using the objection process. In cases where a stay of execution is
   granted by an administrative court, the relevant authorities must be
   notified in writing within one day. For housing-related disciplinary
   decisions, objections to removal from dormitories must be filed within
   five working days, and the Disciplinary Board must decide on such
   objections within seven working days.
------------------------------------------------------------

   The Sabancı University Main Endowment Scholarship Fund is a financial aid
   fund established to provide support to undergraduate and graduate students
   who demonstrate financial need. It is funded through contributions from
   university employees, who can donate a percentage of their gross monthly
   salary via salary deduction, as well as from alumni and other individuals.
   Contributions from employees are processed annually, with informational
   invitations sent in January and donations submitted through a formal form
   or email to the payroll department. The fund also accepts international
   donations via a partnership with the Turkish Philanthropic Fund in New
   York, enabling US-based donors to benefit from tax incentives.

================

🤖 RAG ANSWER (59.0s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   To open a graduate program, the Graduate School Board, under the
   coordination of the Vice President for Education, submits an application
   to the Council of Higher Education (YOK) with the recommendation of the
   relevant board, the opinion of the University Executive Committee, an
   Academic Council decision, and the approval of the Board of Trustees;
   education begins only after YOK approval. For closing a graduate program,
   the Graduate School Board proposes closure with justifications, which the
   Academic Council may approve, request additional information on, or
   reject; if approved, the proposal is forwarded to the Board of Trustees
   for final decision, and upon approval, the university notifies YOK and
   updates student records within one week. Information about closed programs
   is retained in the system to preserve academic history for graduated
   students.
------------------------------------------------------------

[Gpa Calculation]

  • Requirement: last grade for a repeated course is taken into account

🔗 RELATIONS (From Knowledge Graph):

  • Sabancı University Grad...
================

🤖 RAG ANSWER (57.8s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   At Sabancı University, GPA is calculated by multiplying the SU credit of
   each course by its grade coefficient, then dividing the total grade points
   by the total SU credits of all courses taken in that term. For
   undergraduate students, the Cumulative GPA (CGPA) includes all courses
   taken since enrollment, and the last grade for any repeated course is used
   in the calculation. For graduate students, the same method applies to both
   term GPA and CGPA, with all digits after the decimal point considered in
   academic ranking, though displayed with two decimals. Transcripts include
   all registered courses, and repeated courses are accounted for using the
   most recent grade.
------------------------------------------------------------

  • faculty member → [responsible for] → pr...
================

🤖 RAG ANSWER (44.5s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Students may apply for semester or academic year leave due to health,
   financial, family, personal, academic reasons, or unexpected indispensable
   situations, provided they submit supporting documentation. For
   undergraduate students, if the leave request is submitted by the last
   business day of the 4th week after classes begin, they must pay 25% of the
   semester or annual tuition fee; if submitted between the 5th and 7th week,
   they must pay 50%; if submitted between the 8th and 10th week, they must
   pay the full fee. Students who enrolled in the 2020–2021 academic year or
   later and request leave during their first year must pay the full tuition
   fee regardless of timing. Graduate students may apply for semester leave
   under similar conditions, and if they return early, the leave fee paid is
   deducted from their next semester’s tuition, subject to approval by the
   Graduate School Administrative Board. Students conscripted, arrested,
   condemned, or taken into custody may apply within two months of the event
   to receive leave retroactive to the start of the situation, subject to
   institutional approval.
------------------------------------------------------------

   Formu ile başvuruda bulunursunuz. Başvurular, akademik takvimde ilan
   edilen süre içinde, ders kayıtları başlamadan önce yapılır ve en fazla 93
   SÜ kredisine sahip olan öğrenciler başvuruda bulunabilir. Ayrıca, geçiş
   yapmak istediğiniz programın kontenjanı ve kabul koşulları fakülte yönetim
   kurulunca belirlenir ve bu koşullar sizlere bildirilir.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (42.7s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Disciplinary penalties for students include warning, reprimand, temporary
   suspension from one week to one month, suspension for one or two
   semesters, and dismissal from the university. For offenses leading to
   warning, reprimand, or short-term suspension, disciplinary proceedings
   must begin within one month of the supervisor becoming aware; for longer
   suspensions or dismissal, the deadline is three months. Students suspended
   for any duration are prohibited from entering the university campus, must
   pay tuition for the suspended semester, and the suspension period does not
   count toward the maximum study period. Repeat offenses result in a penalty
   one level higher, though dismissal cannot be imposed for repeated
   infractions. Students suspended for at least one semester are also
   referred to psychotherapeutic and psychological services, and their
   scholarship status is reviewed separately.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (56.9s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Prospective graduate students must apply for graduation through the SIS
   system within the dates specified in the academic calendar to initiate the
   audit process. Students are responsible for tracking their completed
   courses, credits, and remaining requirements via SIS, and only those
   registered for the semester or participating in an exchange program can be
   considered for graduation. The Institutes, with approval from the Director
   of Graduate School, submit lists of eligible candidates to the Student
   Resources unit after verifying that all academic conditions—including
   course completion, credit thresholds, thesis requirements, and GPA—are
   met. Students who do not qualify in a given semester must reapply for the
   next one, and those who fail to register due to disciplinary action or
   leave are ineligible. Upon approval, diplomas are issued in both Turkish
   and English, signed by the President and the Director of Graduate School
   on the front, and the Director of Student Resources on the back.

🔗 RELATIONS (From Knowledge Graph):

================

🤖 RAG ANSWER (68.1s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Anyone who finds an item on campus must report it by calling extension
   3565 or delivering it to the Security Office in Building D2. Security
   officers then complete a two-copy Found Item Return Form, sign it with the
   finder, and keep the original while giving the second copy to the finder.
   Found items are numbered upon receipt by the Security Unit and categorized
   as valuable or non-valuable, with each item placed in a labeled bag.
   Unclaimed items are retained for one year, after which they may be
   disposed of. In case of loss or theft of personal items, individuals must
   notify the Security Unit by phone (5555) and submit a written report via
   the IT Unit’s ticketing system. Subcontractor and tenant companies are
   required to deliver forgotten items to the Security Unit on the same day
   they are found.
------------------------------------------------------------

------------------------------------------------------------
   Special event and catering services at Sabancı University must be arranged
   by submitting the Special Event and Catering Request Form at least 30 days
   before the event through SUForm. The requesting unit is responsible for
   ensuring timely submission, as delays may cause disruptions. Food menus
   and specifications for events such as meals, coffee breaks, and cocktails
   are prepared by the Food and Beverage Services unit upon request, and no
   advance procurement is allowed without this formal process. The university
   evaluates potential service providers every three years based on hygiene,
   service quality, equipment, personnel, and references, and only approved
   companies may be selected after a procurement proposal is reviewed and
   approved.

------------------------------------------------------------
   Students at Sabancı University are covered by health insurance from the
   start of the academic term until graduation, excluding Exchange and
   Project Management Undergraduate students. They can access on-campus
   health services including preventive screenings such as chest X-ray,
   complete blood count, Hepatitis A, Hepatitis B, and MMR tests during
   enrollment, with results recorded in the Health Information Form. Students
   may request appointments for psychiatric or psychological evaluations by
   phone or email, and can receive prescribed medications from the Health
   Center by email request or in person. If referred by a Health Center
   physician, students can receive private health services at contracted
   institutions, though no referral is needed for inpatient treatments like
   surgery. Emergency services are provided by a fully equipped ambulance
   team, and cases are transported to the nearest announced pandemic hospital
   when necessary. Health services are not valid abroad, and foreign students
   have a 40% participation rate in coverage.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (52.0s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The Rectorate Research Fund is available only to full-time faculty members
   who have been at Sabancı University for at least six months and started
   their position within the last two years; faculty members may apply only
   once. Applicants must ensure their proposed project aligns with their
   existing or ongoing research activities. By the end of the project, the
   Project Manager must have submitted at least two new documented external
   grant applications or secured at least one guaranteed external funding
   source, and must have published high-impact scientific articles. The fund
   supports infrastructure development, with a maximum budget of 300,000 TL
   for theoretical research and 600,000 TL for experimental research, and
   projects cannot exceed 36 months in duration.
------------------------------------------------------------
